## Current Root23 And Preview22 Sitemap Gate

Root23 was rebuilt locally from the exact 76 production inputs recorded for
preview22. Node 22.23.1 ran `ev3e2-build.cjs 23 branded-production`; that argument
selects the existing root-path configuration only. No branded-domain access or
deployment occurred. `npm ci`, `npm audit`, `npm audit --omit=dev`, lint, clean
production build and TypeScript all passed. Both audits reported zero
vulnerabilities. Next 16.3.0, React/ReactDOM 19.2.4, TypeScript 5.9.3, Playwright
1.60.0 and Sharp 0.35.3 are unchanged; package and lockfile hashes match.

A new temporary preservation helper, `ev3e2-root-refresh-build22.cjs`, pins the
preview22 source identity and preserves old root20. The older helper and evidence
remain unchanged. The complete preview export was restored byte-for-byte:
7,152 files, 713,055,109 bytes, inventory SHA-256
`49fbd573a5a73a6ddd2aadffd52ebeac5ae2a2d2c0af1953a9a38b6e57a100b0`.
Old root20 remains in `ev3e2-root-refresh-23/previous-root20` with its original
inventory hash. New root23 has 7,152 files and inventory SHA-256
`7172a8c8adf38e04bb3febff38d57c1a13af73c33d338d4a012a8e30c09dc1b5`.
Preservation report `ev3e2-root-refresh-23/after.json` has SHA-256
`f6e2f95baa13bcb9253398e73d092e93186f7e8c9976234016dbdd9001391243`.
Only the two saved generated preview files were restored, after checking saved
hashes and matching the freshly generated root bytes. No owner files were used.

The local servers were restarted hidden at
`http://127.0.0.1:4214/evaready-electrical/` and `http://127.0.0.1:4218/`.
HTML, sitemap XML, robots and version-marker HTTP responses matched exported
bytes and expected MIME types. The preview origin-root probe correctly returned
404; it was an intentional containment check, not an unexplained resource error.

Current sitemap runs all passed: four cases each in Chromium 148.0.7778.96,
Firefox 150.0.2 and Windows WebKit 26.4 for each configuration, 24 passed and zero
failed, skipped or retried. Run IDs are
`a8-b22-preview-sitemap-desktop-<engine>-1440-01` and
`a8-b23-root-sitemap-desktop-<engine>-1440-01`. Commands use `--suite=sitemap`,
the matching `--profile` and `--id`, `--run-after-primary-approval`, and
`--root-build` only for root23. Exact expanded commands are retained in each
run's `command.json`. Pointer and Enter activation, normal/enlarged text, 44px
targets, exact XML document bytes, href/order and no invalid prefetch path passed.
Keyboard cases seed visible focus before trusted Enter; they are not natural
Tab traversal or physical Safari tests.

Strict reconciliation `ev3e2-a8-root23-preview22-complete-01.json` passed with
zero findings; SHA-256
`2a87cc38346fb4973000ba78f4bfbf2c9d386c5bfc0e5d0b6b4bd990bedaa1b9`.
All 2,108 first-party observations match authorised local forwards. All 24
outside observations were contained, with zero unauthorised forwards or
unexplained runtime errors and no inactive-test source substitutions.

The initial planning command was rejected before execution because PowerShell
array concatenation produced malformed arguments. Explicit separate arguments
fixed the invocation; all six plans then passed. No assertion or containment
rule was changed, and no failed browser run was discarded.

The natural keyboard and current root/preview sitemap gates are complete for
this candidate. Broader current-source layout, scrolling and interaction gates,
register isolation, staged privacy and clean-commit evidence remain incomplete.
Next is the existing 462-case selected-route layout matrix on preview22.
No source, dependency, header, artwork, register, main or deployment change was
made in this root-refresh batch. The phase remains uncommitted.

## Preview22 Complete Main Layout Matrix

Run `a7-b22-full-layout-matrix-chromium-01` passed all 462 cases, with zero
failures, skips, retries or flaky results. This is the exact selected 21-route
matrix at widths 320, 360, 390, 430, 768, 820, 1024, 1366, 1440, 1920 and 2560,
with 100% and simulated 200% root text (16px and 32px). Viewport height is 844
through width 430 and 1080 at wider widths. Every tested pathname retains the
`/evaready-electrical/` preview prefix. The local base URL was
`http://127.0.0.1:4214/evaready-electrical/`.

Node 22.23.1 and Playwright 1.60.0 ran Chromium 148.0.7778.96 on Windows.
The reviewed invocation was `ev3e2-test-drafts/run-focused.cjs`
`--id=a7-b22-full-layout-matrix-chromium-01`
`--profile=desktop-chromium-1440 --suite=matrix --run-after-primary-approval`.
The expanded Node/Playwright command is retained in that run's `command.json`.
The test uses real main content, controls and loaded hero images; it does not
replace public content with fixtures. External integrations remain contained.

Independent cell proof `ev3e2-b22-full-layout-cells-01.json` reports
`PASS-EXACT-LAYOUT-MATRIX`, SHA-256
`15fb7f1c49c1a754ba7c1789ef0c861d0a6fa6327cf6b04933fb97d64557a403`.
It verifies all 462 unique route/width/text cells, exact viewport and font sizes,
complete successful results, renderer mapping, non-vacuous text measurements,
44px targets, focus margins and loaded assets. The temporary checker passed its
positive control and rejected 20 independently corrupted synthetic copies,
including missing/duplicate cells, wrong base paths, clipping and unloaded
assets. The original reports and test assertions were not altered.

The matrix measured 83,974 text-node observations, 195,283 text fragments,
6,578 CTA target observations and 462 loaded hero-image observations. Maximum

## Mobile Menu Reflow Repair

The subsequent navigation run exposed two real compact-menu defects. At 320px
with doubled root text, an implicit grid column grew to 304.328px inside 270px
of available width. The menu's scroll width reached 316px against a 294px client
width, clipping a group-toggle focus outline. The A3-scoped correction gives the
grid a `minmax(0, 1fr)` track, lets its children shrink, and wraps the top-level
Home, About and Contact link labels using the existing group-overview pattern.

The build22 308-case attempt was interrupted after 92 logged passes and six
logged failures. It has no final JSON report or proxy teardown reconciliation
and is not accepted coverage. A four-case temporary CSS comparison reproduced
the defect and passed with the proposed treatment; injected diagnostic CSS is
not production acceptance. The actual build24 narrow run passed 56 browser
cases, but its evidence review found that the deferred Service Areas search
input arrived after target-size measurements. That result does not prove the
search target was checked.

The navigation test now explicitly waits for the real searchbox before measuring
all controls. Its build24 rerun passed 54 and failed two cases, both Service Areas
at 320px/doubled text: the input measured 43.21875px wide by 96px high. Nested
rem-based padding and gap consumed the available width. Build25 caps only this
mobile navigation search's horizontal padding and gap at their normal values;
text and icon scaling remain intact. The 44px minimum target assertion, focus,

### Firefox Dropdown Focus Repair In Progress

The next build26 profile, `a3-b26-full-navigation-desktop-firefox-1440-01`,
completed with 14 passes and 14 failures, zero skips/retries. The six-profile
queue stopped before starting its other five profiles. All failures are clipped
natural keyboard focus in scrolling desktop dropdowns. The failed attempt's
network reconciliation reports only the failed/incomplete functional result,
with no additional containment or identity findings; it is not a passing run.

Two observation-only Level 2 dropdown cases at 1440x900 and 1440x1080 with
doubled root text tested the timing hypothesis. Firefox clipping persisted over
45 additional animation frames with unchanged panel scroll positions. At 900px,
Consumer mains ended at 723.583px beyond the panel's 707.433px bottom. At 1080px,
three controls remained clipped. Matching Chromium cases had no early or settled
clipping at either height. Both diagnostic runs separately passed request
reconciliation. This rules out the root-only settle helper as the explanation
for these observations; no focus assertion was weakened.

The bounded A3 correction measures the focused control and open panel after
native scrolling, then makes at most one instant correction to the panel only.
It never changes keyboard focus, window scroll, history or activation. Delayed
work is cancelled or rejected after new input/focus, navigation, overlay ownership
or unmount. No banner, artwork, ticker, navigation wording, destinations, order,
or existing close/open logic changes. A new isolated handler test exercises 33
geometry/ownership scenarios and two source-isolation constraints. These mocked
unit checks supplement, and do not replace, natural browser traversal.

The first unit-harness attempt lacked the CommonJS module container and compared
mixed-line-ending source bytes incorrectly. Both were repaired: the isolation
test now checks exact approved A3/A5 additions against the committed baseline,
including a separate hash of the unchanged A5 measurement block. All 35 focused
tests then passed. Build attempt27 passed clean install and both npm audits, but
lint rejected the test variable name `module`. It was renamed without changing
assertions. Attempt27 remains failed; fresh build28 validation is in progress.

The build26 observations and failures are retained, never relabelled as build28
acceptance. New production build, static checks, natural navigation matrices,
request reconciliation and all remaining Phase3E2 gates are still required.

### Build30 Windows WebKit Compositing Correction

The next reversible diagnostic isolated the repaint trigger. With the original
DPR3 profile, pausing the only active animation, `ev-marquee-scroll`, reduced
median native frame intervals from 776ms to 30ms at 1024px and from 1783ms to
30ms at 2560px. Resuming it reproduced 771ms and 1777ms respectively. Removing
the observed backdrop/filter declarations did not materially alter the result.
The ticker itself is protected and was not changed in source or export.
`ev3e2-webkit-paint-diagnostic-04-summary.json` has SHA256
`02e100a7af1ed14864f68d8bc59ef46eb82cf757e19cc087306ccd40818ec6bc`.
Its six diagnostic contexts reconcile with no network findings; proof SHA256
`54ef1192a6eefc2aefd6aeed444009f4ef59773efae8091dcd9d2198a62eb5fd`.

Inspection of installed Playwright 1.60.0 `coreBundle.js` showed its Windows
WebKit default launch adds `--disable-accelerated-compositing`. Diagnostic05
compared that default with omission of only that argument, retaining the same
mobile profile, DPR3, normal ticker animation, complete tracing, timeouts,
source/export and both containment layers. Actual launch arguments were retained
in the browser debug log and checked exactly, not inferred from the config.
At 1024px, default/composited/composited/default median frame intervals were
780/45/46/785ms. At 2560px, default/composited intervals were 1783/46ms. The
default's scroll/screenshot timeouts remain recorded; all composited diagnostic
operations finished within the original 10-second action limit.

The measured failure therefore has an environment cause: the forced software
compositor makes the unchanged animated ticker expensive at high pixel density.
The correction is a temporary Windows WebKit launch configuration, not a ticker
rewrite, reduced-motion substitution, reduced-DPR test or relaxed assertion.
This is Windows lab evidence, not physical Safari or field performance.
Diagnostic05 summary SHA256:
`c7e14c753c129f3c4176a6b97ecd4ff4c5ef6be1a1459f219acc98db26088862`.
Its six contexts reconcile with no network findings; proof SHA256:
`5a218c1cbced1f83e84b1a512b77ad65dac322b613daff32c9c0dac577d22b2d`.

PNG signature, dimensions and nonblank pixels were verified. The retained 1024px
default/composited header captures have the same artwork/navigation composition;
their moving ticker naturally has a different phase. The composited 2560px
header is rendered and nonblank. This limited visual review does not replace
the required complete geometry, keyboard and functional matrix.

The new supplemental harness `ev3e2-header-compositing-01` omits only that one
argument, on Windows and four named WebKit profiles. Fourteen regression tests
verify exact preservation of all other profile, proxy, tracing and timing
options, rejection of other engines/platforms, and rejection of an existing

`e0718e99a75892779010a15e091b11cdcda945f647bc7ef123c716a7c7076933`.
